# Skills · A · part 52

807 entries, adversarial-review to advisor-decision. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **adversarial-review**: The procedure for adversarially reviewing a change — resolve what is under review, attack it, return a verdict. Its job is to break the change, not to approve it. Loaded by the `adversary` subagent when the review gate or Phase 5 spawns it, and usable directly on any diff. ([Vavly/skills](https://github.com/Vavly/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/spec-gate/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: 差分と前提仕様を、実装者から独立したコンテキストで監査する。 「レビューして」「敵対レビュー」「差分レビュー」で発動。 ([Vigener/dotfiles](https://github.com/Vigener/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: Adversarial code review. Spawns one reviewer agent per critical lens to challenge work from a distinct angle. Produces a synthesized verdict with findings and lead judgment. Triggers: "adversarial review". ([WAB-Studio/meeting-transcriber-net](https://github.com/WAB-Studio/meeting-transcriber-net/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: Loop adversarial de revision de codigo con multiples agentes. Lanza reviewers con incentivos para romper el codigo, aplica fixes, re-testea, y repite hasta 0 bugs. Usar despues de features grandes o antes de releases. NOT for style reviews or minor changes. ([webcomunicasolutions/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/webcomunicasolutions/claude-code-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: Use when a plan or spec is drafted and needs independent review before approval or execution, countering the authoring session's commitment bias. Triggers on "adversarial review", "adversary review", "independent review of this plan/spec", "second opinion on the spec", "red-team this plan", "get fresh eyes on this", "stress-test this spec" — and proactively at plan-complete or spec-complete boundaries before the user approves. For plans and specs (design docs, implementation plans, ADR sets), not code diffs — code correctness goes to the code-review skills, code structure to nuclear-code-revi… ([xinye1/xl-skills](https://github.com/xinye1/xl-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: 三角色对抗式代码评审（互相审判）——挑刺者带证据指控 → 辩护者默认"挑刺者是错的"独立复核裁定 → 改进者只修裁定成立项并配能变红的回归判据。触发条件：执行 piv-review-changes（审查门禁）时的默认执行协议；用户说"对抗式评审/三角色评审/互相审判/挑刺再复核"；critical/high 判定有争议或"审查通过未发现问题"被质疑时。 ([xuejianqiang123/CStackGUI](https://github.com/xuejianqiang123/CStackGUI/tree/HEAD/.codebuddy/skills/t3-quality/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: Runs a single-critic attack on an implementation plan before execution. Checks the plan across six fixed failure lenses and returns a STOP, REVISE, or SHIP verdict. Trigger with /adversarial-review, "red team this plan", "poke holes", "break this plan", or "find holes". ([yang-l/configs](https://github.com/yang-l/configs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adversarial-review))
- **adversarial-review**: Strict, read-only multi-angle pre-commit review protocol for auditing code changes against security boundaries, backward compatibility, correctness, and architectural invariants. ([Youssef1473/Jarvis2.0](https://github.com/Youssef1473/Jarvis2.0/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adversarial-review))

- **adversarial-review-against-dev**: Review the current branch against origin/dev as a skeptical reviewer, verify each finding, fix the clear defects as new self-contained commits, and ask about findings that may be intended or need a design decision. Use for an adversarial or pre-PR review of a branch. Do not use for existing PR review comments (resolve-review-comments), failing CI (fix-github-ci), security scans (security-scan), or HTML review pages (html-commit-review). ([j3soon/nurse-scheduling](https://github.com/j3soon/nurse-scheduling/tree/HEAD/skills/adversarial-review-against-dev) · ★15)
- **adversarial-review-and-fix**: [Tier 2 · moderate autonomy · full review gate · the proven two-phase workhorse] Run a rigorous CODE-GROUNDED adversarial architecture review of a repo, FREEZE it as the source of truth, then close every confirmed finding one at a time until done. Use for a security/architecture/ reliability hardening pass, a pre-production audit-and-remediate, or "review the whole app and fix everything." Phase 0 reviews the actual source (not existing docs) and a skeptic narrows out false findings; Phase 1 fixes the confirmed set with full safety rails. Runs via the autonomous-fleet-core engine. - **adversarial-review-blind**: 기획·계약 문서를 적대적으로 리뷰해 결함을 전량 보고한다. 블라인드 이중 리뷰어 + 메인 독립 검증 하네스이며, 문서 종류(리서치·후보·심사·문제정의·계약·결정기록)마다 다른 공격축을 쓴다. 기획·설계·계약 문서를 새로 쓰거나 크게 고친 뒤, 되돌리기 비싼 하류 작업(구현·배선·심사)에 진입하기 전에 사용. - **adversarial-review-change**: Change-mode review procedure for the adversarial-reviewer agent. Fires when the target is a code diff, GitHub PR URL, or working-tree diff. - **adversarial-review-code**: Use when you need to adversarially review a code diff or PR for correctness bugs, silent failures, and type design problems, and architecture conformance (dependency direction, earned abstraction). Dispatches pr-review-toolkit agents in parallel, consolidates findings into CRITICAL / IMPORTANT / MINOR severity buckets, and optionally auto-dispatches a single fixer. Complements /adversarial-review-doc, which checks document integrity rather than code. ([GarrettManley/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/GarrettManley/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/docs/skills/adversarial-review-code))
- **adversarial-review-consolidate**: Merge several adversarial-review reports for one pull request (from different teammates and models) into one deduplicated PR review with an inline thread per finding, where the PR author records fix / ignore / defer decisions as replies. Also has a `misses` mode for after human review: list what human reviewers found that the AI reviews missed. Use when a PR author asks to consolidate, merge, or triage adversarial reviews, asks what pre-review missed, or asks how pre-review works or what to do next on a PR. ([bcgov/aps-devops](https://github.com/bcgov/aps-devops/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adversarial-review-consolidate))
- **adversarial-review-cycle**: User-invoked only. Do not auto-select. - **adversarial-review-doc**: Use when you need to adversarially review any markdown document for structural problems, broken cross-references, stale claims, terminology drift, or placeholder text. Runs dimension agents in parallel, consolidates findings into CRITICAL / IMPORTANT / MINOR severity buckets, and optionally auto-dispatches a fixer. Complementary to /reviewer-personas, which checks team-member perspectives rather than document integrity. ([GarrettManley/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/GarrettManley/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/docs/skills/adversarial-review-doc))
- **adversarial-review-engine**: Two modes of ruthless critical analysis. Prosecution mode dismantles someone else's writing, argument, or claim as a hostile peer-reviewer and opposing counsel — auditing every fact, quote, citation, and statistic against primary sources, delivering a severity-ranked Kill List, steelman counter-narrative, verdicts, and concessions. Devil's-advocate mode turns the same weapons on the user's own position, draft, or point of view to make it more robust — surfacing the strongest objections an opponent would raise, then hardening the position and restating it at full strength. Use when the user wa… ([fbhadha/Skills](https://github.com/fbhadha/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adversarial-review-engine))
- **adversarial-review-fast**: Use when a small code change needs the cheapest adversarial review — "a fast adversarial review", "a one-reviewer review", "just review what changed since the last review", "re-check the fixes" — a change that stays inside one subsystem, often right after a larger review whose fixes need checking. Also use mid-task, without being asked, before building on your own understanding of how some code or system behaves, so one unbiased agent tries to disprove it first. For a bigger change or to pick reviewers use `adversarial-review-quick`; for a thorough review use `adversarial-review`. ([patrickdappollonio/claude-plugins](https://github.com/patrickdappollonio/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/adversarial-review/skills/adversarial-review-fast) · ★13)
- **adversarial-review-feedback**: Use this skill when the user wants review feedback on a GitHub PR acted on, whether it comes from Claude, Copilot, CodeRabbit, SonarQube or a human reviewer, and whether it's on the PR or pasted in. It checks every claim against the code, git history and library sources, fixes only what holds up, logs each outcome in the PR description, then commits and pushes. If the PR has no review yet, it triggers the AI reviewer first. Use it for requests like "we got some PR feedback", "more feedback", "fix the review comments" or "handle the review on PR 123", even when the user doesn't name the review… ([BrianEstrada/diffcop](https://github.com/BrianEstrada/diffcop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adversarial-review-feedback))
- **adversarial-review-gate**: Run the mandatory skeptic review over a staged diff and record it in the PR body. Use before `gh pr create` on any change touching Go code in core-agent. ([go-steer/core-agent](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adversarial-review-gate) · ★9)
- **adversarial-review-gate**: Adversarial code review that tries to break a change instead of approving it, and only counts a finding when a failing test reproduces it, ending in a GATE PASS or GATE FAIL verdict. Use when reviewing a diff, pull request or freshly AI-generated code for race conditions, idempotency, partial-failure, boundary and security bugs, or when you need a review step that cannot rubber-stamp. ([Ces107/agent-forge](https://github.com/Ces107/agent-forge/tree/HEAD/plugins/agent-forge/skills/adversarial-review-gate) · 2 copies)
- **adversarial-review-gauntlet**: Run a sequential adversarial review (G1-G4) over a finished feature before declaring it done — G1 mechanical correctness (spec trio complete, all tasks checked, traceability gap-free via scripts/gauntlet.sh), G2 adversarial behavior, G3 maintainability, G4 evidence with a written report. A G1 REJECT stops everything. Use after the last implementation batch, before a completion gate or final human approval, when self-reviewing a feature, or when asked to gauntlet, adversarially review, or harden a feature. ([jordimarsal/harness](https://github.com/jordimarsal/harness/tree/HEAD/skills/adversarial-review-gauntlet) · ★1)
- **adversarial-review-lenses**: Use when reviewing a phase artifact — a discovery brief, a plan, a design, an acceptance specification, or a slice reported as delivered — and the review has to end in a defensible pass-or-block verdict rather than a list of remarks. Covers judging whether the artifact covers everything that fed into it, whether it matches the business intent, whether it holds together internally, and what it puts at risk downstream, then weighing those into a single call. Not for routine code review of an ordinary change. - **adversarial-review-loop**: Break your own change before a reviewer does. An adversarial review loop whose subagents try to REFUTE the diff rather than bless it, with a round budget, fixes applied at the class rather than the site, mutation-proved tests, and named exits from a loop that has stopped converging. Use before pushing a branch or opening a PR, while delivering a feature, when a review gate keeps returning findings on the same change, or on any request to review adversarially, attack, break or harden a change. - **adversarial-review-loop**: unworklet の実装コードを、独立した subagent 群による批判的レビュー → agent 自身のトリアージ → 判断が要る項目だけ余湖さんに質問 → TDD で自律 fix → 再レビュー、を「指摘ゼロ or トリアージ結果が要対応ゼロ」に収束するまで回す収束ループ。「バッチレビューして」「adversarial review 回して」「codex-review-loop と同じ手法で」「コードベース全体を客観的に見てもらって」「実装を徹底的に監査して」「review loop 回して」「収束するまでレビュー」「subagent に客観レビューさせて」等で必ず起動。単一実装者バイアス・八百長テスト・既存バグを独立視点で潰すためのもの。対象は実装コード (packages/*/src)。判断基準はプロダクトの方向性 (設計時の spec 文書は削除済みで、照合先は実装と `skills/unworklet/`)。1〜2 file の軽い確認や grep ベースの check では起動せず、コードベース規模の批判的監査ループでのみ走る。 ([yuichkun/unworklet](https://github.com/yuichkun/unworklet/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adversarial-review-loop) · ★11)
